RESIDUAL ANALYSIS
======================================================================
[OK] Saved: result6b_residual_plots.png

DeliveryTime outliers (|residual| > 2 std = 13.04s): 9 of 210 test rows
  Of these, 3 have Actual_DeliveryTime == 120.0s (the simulation's max-time fallback - the 0.15mm penetration target was never reached within the 120s window, which is a real physical outcome for slow-transport parameter combinations, not a model failure). This is why DeliveryTime has the lowest R2 of the four outputs: it is a censored/thresholded quantity (many rows pinned at exactly 120.0), which is harder to regress than a smooth continuous quantity like MaxConcentration.
